**Today (2026-06-29) shipped:** 14 commits + 13 production deploys (`dms-00208-jvz` → `dms-00220-66r`) covering a launch-day arc that closed the security-incident response, brought Manual-flow trial signups live, hardened the admin hosting/order surfaces, scrubbed test-data residue, and shipped 4 separate UX/feedback fixes on top — in chronological order: **(a) Security incident response (BEFORE the day's planned work)**: MongoDB password (`pawan`/`FhbM8sWvpwTZSlIZ`) was leaked into the initial git commit and stayed in history; rotated in Atlas first → `gcloud secrets versions add MONGODB_URI` to Secret Manager v2 → forced new Cloud Run revision with the rotated secret → ran `git filter-branch` over 6000+ commits + 147 tags + force-pushed origin/main + force-pushed all tags. Also shipped `.husky/pre-commit` + `scripts/check-staged-for-secrets.sh` (`a5b6272`) — pre-commit hook that grep-scans staged hunks for MongoDB URIs with embedded creds, Razorpay live/test key secrets (24+ chars after prefix), Anthropic `sk-ant-` keys, AWS `AKIA…` keys, GCP `private_key` JSON, PEM blocks, env-var-shape secret assignments. Blocks the commit on match; only `--no-verify` for confirmed false positives. Auto-memory entry `feedback_no_secrets_in_git` documents the hard rule + the correct order if a leak is found (rotate FIRST, then Secret Manager update, then redeploy, THEN history rewrite — never the reverse). **(b) Manual flow LIVE at 06:12Z**: flipped `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=manual` via `gcloud run services update --update-env-vars` to bring trial signups live under the Manual path while Razorpay UPI Autopay sub-activation (~2026-07-08) is pending. Customers can now click "Start 15-Day Free Trial" → no Razorpay mandate-creation step → trial provisioned end-to-end via DA cron → on day 14 we email a payment-collection reminder + the customer pays via standard one-shot UPI/card. Net-net: signup conversion is not gated on Razorpay's still-pending UPI Autopay activation. Reversal is one command: `gcloud run services update dms --update-env-vars=HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=tokens` (zero downtime; existing Manual customers keep their existing flow). **(c) Admin TRIAL/PAID distinction** (`3b1ede8` in `dms-00208-jvz`): prominent TRIAL/PAID pill in column 1, customer-type filter dropdown (`all` | `trial` | `paid`), count chips at the top, filter-aware empty state. **(d) Deploy-script sticky-flag fix** (`6f4c4a0` in `dms-00210-grt`): 06:28Z post-mortem discovered the `--set-env-vars` (replace) semantics + `${HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW:-subscriptions}` fallback caused the next full deploy to silently revert the operator's `manual` flip back to `subscriptions`. Detected via post-deploy env verification within ~2 min; re-flipped via `--update-env-vars` (revision `dms-00210-grt`). Fix: new `read_current_env_var()` helper queries the active revision's env via `gcloud run services describe ... --format=json` + node; resolution order now is (i) current Cloud Run value → (ii) shell env → (iii) `'subscriptions'` first-deploy default. The resolved value is echoed at deploy time BEFORE traffic shifts. **Outcome**: 10 consecutive correct deploys after the fix landed — `dms-00211` through `dms-00220` all printed `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW resolved to: manual` before promotion + `gcloud run services describe` confirmed `manual` post-deploy. **(e) Pagination UX** (`6f5cdb1` in `dms-00211-zmm`): React 18 `useTransition` wraps `setCurrentPage`, opacity dip during reconciliation, disabled buttons during pending, scroll-to-top via `tableTopRef.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'})`, pulsing "…" indicator next to "Page X of Y". Three signals total that the click registered. **(f) Fetch-loop fix** (`3b7194f` in `dms-00212-sjj`): NextAuth's `refetchOnWindowFocus: true` default returned new session OBJECT references on every focus → useEffect deps treated it as a deps change → re-fired `fetchHostingData` → ~2s loop of "10 rows → 32 rows" every window-focus. Fix: `hasFetchedHostingsRef` (a `useRef`) ensures `fetchHostingData()` runs EXACTLY ONCE per page-mount. **(g) Trial-eligibility mode-gating** (`d374175` in `dms-00213-5rp`): `app/api/user/hosting/trial-eligibility/route.ts` Layer-4 check required `plan.razorpayPlans?.yearly` regardless of mandate mode → after the Manual flip, EVERY "Start Free Trial" click hit "This plan is not available for a free trial" toast. Fix: `if (mandateMode === "subscriptions" && !plan.razorpayPlans?.yearly)` mode-gates the Razorpay-yearly check; the plan-exists check stays mode-independent. 3 new tests pin the fix (manual/tokens with no yearly → eligible; manual + plan missing entirely → still NOT eligible). **(h) Admin-bypass shipped + reverted** (`e012288` + `4f437e3` across `dms-00214-fdj` → `dms-00215-rr6`): operator's own IP+device hit IP_THROTTLE / DEVICE_THROTTLE / one-trial-per-user defenses. Shipped role='admin' carve-out → operator rejected on security grounds ("admin bypass can create vulnerabilities") → reverted within ~5 min. **Net production state**: no admin-bypass code on either route; abuse defenses apply uniformly. Both commits kept in audit trail per CLAUDE.md's "NEVER delete completed tasks" rule. **(i) Deep-link from User Services** (`4234ebc` in `dms-00216-vs9`): added "Manage" column to the Hosting Services table in the `/admin/user-management` user-services modal — `Open ↗` purple pill links to `/admin/hosting?q={encodeURIComponent(domainName)}`. Receiver side (`useSearchParams` in `app/admin/hosting/page.tsx`) seeds initial `searchTerm` from `?q=`. **(j) Two-pass-deeplink fix** (`49604e3` in `dms-00217-t9d`): the deep-link triggered Pass 1 (`?firstPage=10`) which returned 10 rows; if the target row was in the not-yet-loaded tail, "No hosting accounts found" flashed for ~1-2s. Fix: `initialSearchPresent` triggers full-fetch-only path (skip Pass 1) when `searchTerm.trim().length > 0` at fetch time + empty-state message uses `Searching for "X"…` during loading. **(k) Test-user purge** (`e1b51e5` script committed standalone): one-off `scripts/purge-test-users.js` cascade-deletes test users across 7 resource types (RecurringChargeAttempt → PendingHosting → DA accounts via best-effort HTTP → Hosting → TrialClaim → Invoice → Order → User); dry-run by default; refuses no-emails; skips role='admin'. Today's run cleaned 6 Users + 7 Orders + 1 Hosting + their cascade from MongoDB. **(l) DA orphan cleanup**: 1 DA account (`tryprd941e` for `careerlamps2023@gmail.com`/`trypratik.com`) couldn't be deleted via either the script OR the production endpoint — DA returned 401 "Not logged in" despite Cloud Run NAT IP `34.14.59.128` being whitelisted at all 4 DA layers (verified). Deferred follow-up: investigate why `pw` can create but not delete some accounts on the same DA server. **(m) Search-restriction** (`65f99a7` in `dms-00218-g4f`): `/admin/hosting` search box was matching across `domain` + `user.name` + `user.email`, which surfaced every row when an email-domain substring matched (typing "excel" returned all 10 rows because every row had `@exceltechnologies.in`). Filter now matches `domain` only; placeholder text updated. **(n) Pass-2 background-fetch UX feedback** (`29038cd` in `dms-00220-66r`): the two-pass-deeplink fix's empty-state "Searching for X…" message was dead code because the outer content-body ternary short-circuited `isDataLoading=true` to skeleton, leaving the inner `isDataLoading && searchTerm` check unreachable. 9 tests), `c42237d` Phase 2F (DA suspend + Hosting.status='expired' + suspension email when MIT charge `RecurringChargeAttempt` reaches MAX_ATTEMPTS=4 — three best-effort actions wrapped in independent try/catch so any single failure doesn't cascade; 3 new tests + 1 enhanced existing test), `5fae71a` Phase 2H (HTTP worker endpoints `/api/workers/tokens-provision-pending/route.ts` + `/api/workers/tokens-charge-recurring/route.ts` mirroring the existing worker pattern — `authorizeCronRequest` + `secureJsonResponse`; defensive guards including `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=tokens` check and `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` live-key check on the charging worker; 9 tests + the 2 `gcloud scheduler jobs create http` commands documented inline in the route file headers for operator copy-paste). Plus 12 docs commits flipping TASKS.md to reflect each phase landing — `ef44d6d`, `11e60a4`, `b278955`, `ff5d403`, `128d36b`, `e9d36f9`, `b3b2926`, `d2ba03c`, `16ec535`, `cd5b0fc`, `22709e6`, `2fb6288`. **End-to-end flow now exists in code** (dormant — all behind `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=subscriptions` default + Cloud Scheduler not yet wired): customer clicks "Start Free Trial" → `/create-order` calls createCustomer + createRecurringTokenOrder (₹2 CIT auth) → frontend opens Razorpay Checkout in recurring-authorization mode (Phase 2G) → customer authorizes mandate → webhook `handleMandateValidationCaptured` fires (Phase 2B) → refunds ₹2 + stores token + creates Hosting (status='pending') (Phase 2C) → within 5-15 min DA-provisioning cron picks up the Hosting + creates DA user + flips `status='active'` + sends welcome email (Phase 2E) → customer can use the hosting → at day 14 MIT cron picks up Hosting + claims a `RecurringChargeAttempt` + calls `chargeViaToken` for the yearly amount (Phase 2D) → on success extends Hosting.expiryDate +1 year, on failure schedules retry with [T+1, T+3, T+7] day backoff and marks 'abandoned' after 4 attempts. **What's still pending before flag can be flipped in production**: Phase 3 (integration tests against Razorpay TEST mode), operator-side run of the one-command setup script `bash scripts/setup-cloud-scheduler-tokens.sh` (creates both Cloud Scheduler jobs idempotently — DA-provision cron every 10 min + MIT-charge cron daily at 22:00 UTC / ~03:30 IST), and operator-side phased flag flip per `docs/razorpay-tokens-migration.md` §5 (Starter tier first; single `gcloud run services update dms --update-env-vars=HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=tokens` command documented at the bottom of the setup script). **Note on today's late-session additions** (after Phase 2H): an Explore-agent self-audit of today's 9 implementation commits caught 2 real HIGH-severity bugs in `lib/services/payment/recurring-charge-service.ts` — (i) race in retry-bump where two concurrent crons could both bump status='failed' → 'in_progress' and double-charge, fixed via atomic `findOneAndUpdate({_id, status:'failed'}, …)` guarded claim (commit `d86dd01`); (ii) money-loss if `hosting.save()` fails after Razorpay charge succeeded, fixed by flipping save order — Hosting first, attempt second — so a Hosting-save failure throws BEFORE marking attempt 'succeeded', engaging the existing retry path naturally (same commit `d86dd01`). Both fixes pinned with new tests. Audit-hit-rate this session: 2 real bugs / 9 audit findings = 22% (the false positives were "looks suspicious but actually fine" cases — webhook signature verification is correctly inherited at the route level, currency conversions are consistent, Razorpay event names match the docs, feature flag is checked at request time not module load). Plus `scripts/setup-cloud-scheduler-tokens.sh` (commit `53c50fd`) — operator-facing one-command tooling that wraps the two `gcloud scheduler jobs create http` commands previously documented inline in the worker route headers; idempotent (re-runs update existing jobs); pulls CRON_SECRET from Secret Manager so the secret never appears in shell history. **Tokens flow is now PRODUCTION-READY IN CODE + HARDENED AGAINST KNOWN RACE/ORDERING BUGS**: every state transition from customer "Start Free Trial" click through CIT auth, ₹2 refund, mandate token storage, Hosting record creation, DA provisioning, day-15 MIT charge (with atomic claim guards + correct save ordering), retry backoff, abandonment, DA suspension, customer dunning notification — AND the HTTP entrypoints Cloud Scheduler needs to invoke the crons on schedule — all exists in code. **Yesterday (2026-06-25) shipped:** 12 commits + 1 deploy on origin/main covering the full Razorpay test→live cutover up to its blocking external dependency PLUS a forward-looking design doc for the "₹2-charge-and-reverse" trial-UX gap — (a) `055c51a` `fix(webhook): rename subscription.payment_failed → subscription.halted` (dead-code event-name bug in `app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:87` — Razorpay never had a `subscription.payment_failed` event; the handler body was correct, just listening for the wrong event string; renamed across route + handler + 3 test files), (b) `5d13978` `feat(scripts): one-off Razorpay LIVE plan regeneration script` (new `scripts/razorpay-regenerate-plans-live.js` — dry-run-by-default, refuses to run unless `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` starts with `rzp_live_`, backs up pre-cutover state before any write, creates 6 plans atomically before touching DB), (c) `ced132d` `docs: Razorpay Tokens migration design doc` (new `docs/razorpay-tokens-migration.md`, ~530 lines — diagnoses the "free trial actually charges money" UX gap as a structural limitation of Razorpay's Subscriptions API and lays out a complete file-by-file migration plan to Razorpay's Tokens API which is what Google / Netflix / Spotify use to show ₹2 instead of full plan price on the mandate-authorization overlay; implementation is 4-5 days of focused work but gated on Razorpay live cutover Steps 4-5 closing first so the parallel-flow migration happens against a verified baseline), and 9 documentation commits flipping TASKS.md as each step landed (`a55e794` Step 1 → Done, `582b907` Step 2 mid-state, `b5194af` Step 2 → Done, `254755c` Focused UI/UX QA pass → Done, `ae3ad29` Step 3 → Done, `59fa028` Step 4 plan checkpoint, `a71aa04` Step 4 paused on UPI Autopay, `8b946fa` eSign activation tracker added, `d898006` stale-header refresh). ## 🏁 Tokens-flow admin-UI audit cycle: CLOSED 26 Jun 2026

Single-day initiative complete. Six audit findings raised against today's freshly-shipped Tokens-flow code — three HIGH-severity (operators were blind to which billing rail a row was on + had no per-attempt visibility into MIT charge state + had no surfacing of recurring-charge failures in integration-health), three MEDIUM-severity (cosmetic at-a-glance triage gaps + a cross-rail timeline view). **6 of 6 findings now CLOSED** via five targeted commits (`a570a7d` + `4da1dd3` + `1198403` + `5f93f85` + `3ed1180`), each pinned with new tests where the code path was previously untested. The original close-out summary below described the state after 5-of-6 had closed; `3ed1180` shipped the final piece (cross-mode `/admin/renewals` dashboard, 16 new tests, 6112/6112 sweep). The 5-of-6 narrative is preserved verbatim below for the audit trail; the Recently Shipped section at the bottom of this file lists `RENEWALS-DASHBOARD:` as the 6th closure.

Five of six findings closed via four targeted commits, each pinned with new tests where the code path was previously untested: `a570a7d` (Order + Hosting detail modals expose `mandateMode` / `razorpayCustomerId` / `razorpayTokenId` / `subscriptionId` / `billingType` / `isTrial` fields and the `/api/admin/hosting/stats` route was extended in lock-step), `4da1dd3` (new `/admin/recurring-charges` dashboard + sidebar nav with status summary cards, filterable table, per-attempt RCA state), `1198403` (integration-health card surfaces recurring-charge failures via direct `RecurringChargeAttempt` query — bulletproof rather than contingent on serverLogger keywords, with two new Razorpay-specific signatures pointing operators at the dashboard), `5f93f85` (mandate-mode badge column on hosting admin table — purple TOKENS / blue SUBSCRIPTION / gray MANUAL + amber TRIAL pill). **One finding deferred as genuine nice-to-have**: cross-mode `/admin/renewals` dashboard — the per-attempt feed at `/admin/recurring-charges` already covers Tokens-flow triage and Razorpay's own dashboard covers Subscriptions-flow; a unified timeline view would mostly duplicate those two surfaces. Tokens-flow admin visibility is now complete across **5 admin surfaces** (hosting table badge column, hosting detail modal, order detail modal, dedicated `/admin/recurring-charges` page, integration-health card). Net new test surface from this audit cycle: 14 tests across 2 new files (`tests/unit/app/api/admin/recurring-charges/route.test.ts` + `tests/unit/app/api/admin/integration-health/route.test.ts` — the latter being the FIRST test coverage the integration-health route ever had); existing tests still pass; full project typecheck clean. **Production safety**: every change is a visible no-op today since `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW=subscriptions` is still default and no Hostings have `razorpayTokenId` populated; the badge column shows MANUAL/SUBSCRIPTION on every existing row, the `/admin/recurring-charges` page shows zero attempts, and the integration-health card adds no new Razorpay signals. Once the operator flips the flag, every surface lights up automatically without further code changes. Ready for the next initiative.

## 🏁 Rescan-4 audit cycle: CLOSED 16 Jun 2026

Eight days of focused safety-check additions are complete. Every production-facing route the system exposes — customer-side, admin-side, public, and machine-only (cron / payment-provider webhooks) — now has automated safety checks that run on every code change and would catch a regression before it can reach customers. 65 vertical slices shipped via 48 production deploys; 5,941 automated checks now run on every commit (up from ~1,300 at the start of the cycle); 4 HIGH-severity dependency vulnerabilities patched along the way. The audit found and fixed real bugs in production along the way (notably the duplicate-TLD display issue in slice 7iB). Nothing currently in flight; ready for the next initiative.

## In Flight

### 🆕 Sub-reseller feature — DESIGN PHASE (operator-requested 2026-08-01, awaiting go-ahead)
- [ ] **Add a sub-reseller option under our own panel** — let our customers act as white-label resellers, onboarding + selling domains/hosting to their OWN end-customers through our UI. **Design done + impact assessed 2026-08-01; NO code yet; awaiting operator decision on when to start.** **Recommended model: Model A ("our-own" white-label layer)** — a new `role: "reseller"` + `Reseller`/`ResellerCustomer` models, a markup/margin engine over our existing wholesale prices, a prepaid **wallet** for settlement (simplest; Razorpay **Route** split-payments as a later option), a scoped `/reseller/*` panel, and orders that provision through our **existing** single RC-account + DA-server machinery tagged with `resellerId` (no new RC/DA integration). Rejected Model B (native RC sub-reseller + DA reseller accounts) as heavier + not "under our own panel" — noted as a later upgrade for large resellers. **Impact on current progress: none if additive + flag-gated** — the `role` enum only gains a value, `resellerId` is nullable on Order/Hosting/Domain (existing rows stay valid), and all current customer/admin flows + this session's work are orthogonal. The one hard rule: **airtight `resellerId` query-scoping** (a reseller must never see another tenant's data). **Recommendation: finish the Razorpay TEST→LIVE cutover + launch FIRST, then build sub-reselling as a Phase-2 revenue feature** (it's weeks of work; doing it first delays go-live). **Restore point taken before any of this**: `restore-2026-08-01-pre-sub-reseller` git tag + Cloud Run `dms-00452-z2x` + image tags `stable-2026-08-01`/`known-good-pre-sub-reseller` (see RESTORE_POINTS.md). Phased rollout (each shippable + reversible): (1) role + model + admin-create/approve; (2) read-only scoped reseller panel; (3) markup + wallet; (4) reseller-initiated orders that provision + debit wallet; (5) settlement/payouts + light branding.

### ⚠️ Paid hosting without a linked domain fails to provision (needs product decision, 2026-07-29)
- [x] **✅ RESOLVED on 2026-07-29 (`dms-00441-d7x`) — chose Option A (require a linked/registered domain before hosting checkout).** A paid **hosting** order could complete + invoice but then **fail DA provisioning** with `Cannot Create Account - Invalid Domain Name` when **no real domain was linked** — the hosting item carried a placeholder domain (`hosting-Plus-<ts>`), which DA rejects. Surfaced on a guest paid-hosting test (order `ord_1785303670791_oxjhlz`, INV-000030 created, ₹2246.40 charged in test, hosting **not** provisioned). Fix: both `create-order` and `guest/create-order` now infer the linked domain when the cart has exactly one domain item, then reject the whole order up front (400 `HOSTING_DOMAIN_REQUIRED`, clear actionable message) if any hosting item still lacks a real domain (empty, `hosting-` placeholder, or no dot). The logged-in cart already blocks unlinked hosting client-side; guest checkout surfaces the 400 as a toast + resets state (no stuck screen). Trials are covered by the same guard. See "FIXED: paid hosting requires a real domain" in Recently Shipped.

### 🚦 Razorpay TEST → LIVE cutover checklist (operator — the last go-live gate, 2026-07-28)
The token-based 15-day trial is verified end-to-end in **TEST mode** (signup → ₹2 mandate → refund → provision → active → cancel-with-teardown, all with emails). Before real customers, run this on the **live** Razorpay account:

- [ ] **Verify UPI Autopay / Netbanking eMandate / eSign appear in the *live* mandate checkout** — the trial opens Razorpay with `recurring:'1'`, so only recurring-capable, activated rails show. In TEST mode only **card eMandate** is available (sandbox limitation), so this can ONLY be verified in live. Operator reports these are activated on the live account; confirm they surface in the live mandate overlay (and that UPI Autopay is enabled specifically for *Subscriptions/Recurring*, not just one-time UPI). If a rail is missing, request its per-method recurring activation in the Razorpay dashboard (NPCI/UIDAI-processed, days-to-weeks).
- [ ] **Confirm a paid one-shot order** (domain/paid hosting) issues a **Zoho invoice** in live (trials correctly issue none).
- [ ] After a clean live verification, wipe any live test artifacts (test user + its DA account, via the standard scoped-delete procedure).

Set `DIRECTADMIN_IP=34.93.167.160` (server1's primary IP, = the panel's resolved IP). After the fix the trial provisioned cleanly (`activated:1`, `testing.com` → active, DA user `testi5491c`). ### Mobile-compatibility audit + fixes (operator-requested 2026-07-17)

Operator: *"Make sure our app is mobile compatible. Run comprehensive test and give me a report."* A comprehensive static responsiveness audit was run across all four surfaces of the app (57 pages total: 21 public/marketing/auth/checkout, 13 user-dashboard, 23 admin) plus the shared chrome and global CSS/Tailwind primitives, targeting 360–414px phones. **Fundamentals OK**: Next.js injects the default `width=device-width, initial-scale=1` viewport (no accessibility-harming `maximum-scale`/`user-scalable=no` override), and the recent homepage passes already handle `sm:`/`md:`/`lg:` breakpoints.

- [x] **✅ RESOLVED on 2026-07-30 — all CRITICAL + MAJOR findings remediated across two batches (`dms-00444-z5s` A+B, `dms-00444-cba` C → live `dms-00445-fb2`).** The 2026-07-17 report's fixes are now applied + live. **Batch A+B (`dms-00444-z5s`, customer-facing):** C1 global `overflow-x:hidden` safety net (one line defends every page), C2 bulk-search rows now flex-wrap (were forcing full-page horizontal scroll at 360px), M1 toaster mobile-first full-width (was clipped off-screen), M2 ScrollToTop FAB → `hidden md:block` (no chat-widget collision), M3 HostingUpgradeModal `max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto` (Pay button was clipping), M4 domain-detail nameserver radios stack on mobile, M5 domain-detail title `min-w-0`+`truncate`. **Batch C (commit `dms-00444-cba` → live `dms-00445-fb2`, admin, lowest priority):** M6 DNS action icon buttons p-1.5→p-2.5 (44px tap targets), M7 DNS value column `min-w-0` on mobile so the contained table stops forcing side-scroll. The 10 MINOR polish items are **deferred** (cosmetic, non-breaking — e.g. a couple of `grid-cols-2` register/stat rows without an `sm:` base, sub-44px icon buttons on a few dashboard tables); low value, can be picked up opportunistically. See "MOBILE-AUDIT-REMEDIATION" in Recently Shipped. Original report retained below for the audit trail.
- [x] ~~**MOBILE-AUDIT: comprehensive mobile-compatibility report + remediation**~~ — ⏳ was PENDING (report generated 2026-07-17). Report + method + full findings retained below for the audit trail; remediation shipped 2026-07-30 (see the RESOLVED entry above).

  **Method**: 4 parallel read-only audits (public/marketing/auth/checkout · user-dashboard · admin · global CSS/Tailwind/shared-primitives), 360–414px phone target, ~57 pages + shared chrome.

  **Verdict**: the app is **mobile-first overall** — off-canvas drawer sidebars on BOTH the user dashboard (`UserLayout.tsx`) and admin (`AdminLayout.tsx`); every shared table (`AdminDataTable`, `AdminTable`) wraps in `overflow-x-auto`; the reusable `Modal.tsx` + all one-off renewal/upgrade modals are `w-full` + gutter + `max-h`/scroll (one exception, below); Tailwind mobile-first defaults intact (no custom `screens`); viewport meta is Next's default `width=device-width,initial-scale=1` with NO accessibility-harming `user-scalable=no`. So there is **no systemic breakage** — findings are localized.

  **CRITICAL (2)** — cause page-level horizontal scroll on phones:
  - **C1 · No global `overflow-x-hidden` safety net** — `app/globals.css` (@layer base) + `app/layout.tsx` html/body have no `overflow-x:hidden` / `max-width:100%`. Any single over-wide element anywhere produces full-page horizontal scroll. Highest-leverage fix (one line defends every page). Fix: `html,body{overflow-x:hidden;max-width:100%}` (safe — the header is `fixed`, not an ancestor-dependent `sticky`).
  - **C2 · Bulk-search result rows overflow** — `app/domains/bulk-search/page.tsx:261-310`. Non-wrapping `flex` row whose fixed children (checkbox + `w-20` price + `w-28` action + badge + `px-6` + 4×`gap-4`) sum to ~419px min, so on a 360px screen the domain column is crushed to 0 and the row forces horizontal scroll. Fix: `flex-wrap` + `gap-y-2`, `px-4 sm:px-6`, fixed widths → `sm:w-20`/`sm:w-28`. (Header strip at `:217` shares the pattern.)

  **MAJOR (7)** — awkward/unusable-in-part but not page-breaking:
  - **M1 · Toaster `!important` mispositioning** — `globals.css:308-315` forces `right/bottom:80px !important`, beating the layout's inline `right:24px`; on a 360px phone a ~350px toast anchored 80px in is clipped. Fix: mobile-first `left:16px;right:16px;bottom:16px`, reserve inset for `sm:`+.
  - **M2 · Bottom-left FAB collision** — `ScrollToTop.tsx:39` (z-40) + `ChatWidget.tsx:149` (z-50) both anchor bottom-left on phones (homepage); one covers the other, taps hit wrong control. Fix: distinct corners / vertical stack.
  - **M3 · HostingUpgradeModal not scrollable** — `components/HostingUpgradeModal.tsx:176` has `overflow-hidden` but no `max-h`+`overflow-y-auto`; tall steps clip the Pay/Confirm button off-screen on a 360×640 phone. (Sibling `DomainRenewalModal` does it right.) Fix: add `max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto`.
  - **M4 · Domain-detail nameserver radios don't stack** — `app/dashboard/domains/[id]/page.tsx:197` `flex space-x-4` never stacks; two padded cards crushed to ~150px at 360px. Fix: `flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4`.
  - **M5 · Domain-detail header title overflow** — same file `:157-171`; `text-2xl` domain name with no `truncate`/`min-w-0` in a `justify-between` row can shove/overflow the status pill. Fix: `min-w-0`+`truncate` on h1, `shrink-0` on badge. (This detail page is the least mobile-polished file in the app.)
  - **M6 · Admin dense icon-action clusters** — raw tables in `admin/domains:342`, `admin/dns-management:967/988`, `admin/hosting/packages:239`, `admin/hosting/pending:248` render `p-1.5` (~28px) icon buttons below the 44px tap target. Fix: `p-2.5`/`gap-2` or collapse into the existing `ActionMenu` kebab.
  - **M7 · Admin DNS records table wide scroll** — `admin/dns-management:939/965/986` `min-w-[200px]`/`min-w-[150px]` + `whitespace-nowrap` force ~700px rows; contained in `overflow-x-auto` (no page break) but the operator must side-scroll the primary content to edit. Fix: stacked cards on mobile, or `min-w-0`+`break-all`.

  **MINOR (10, polish)** — ✅ RESOLVED 2026-08-01 (`dms-00448-mn1..mn8`, one commit per item; see "MOBILE-MINOR-POLISH" in Recently Shipped): ~~register name row `grid-cols-2`~~ ✅ mn1 (`grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2`); ~~sub-44px icon buttons on invoices~~ ✅ mn2 (`p-1.5`→`p-2.5`); ~~orders header row no `flex-col sm:flex-row`~~ ✅ mn3; ~~support stat tiles fixed `grid-cols-3`~~ ✅ mn4 VERIFIED FINE (3 compact count-chips read correctly at 3-across; a 2+1 split would look worse — no change); ~~admin DNS sidebar `h-[calc(100vh-14rem)] sticky` not gated to `xl:`~~ ✅ mn5 (gated to `xl:`); ~~admin raw tables `hidden sm:table-cell` column-drop~~ ✅ mn6 DONE 2026-08-01 (`dms-00448-mn6`) — done surgically, not as a blind 11-table sweep: the two main list tables an operator is most likely to open on a phone (admin **domains** + **hosting**) each now hide their most-secondary column below sm (Expiry Date / Usage GB) via `hidden sm:table-cell` on the th AND its matching td (verified balanced). The remaining raw tables (in-modal mini-tables, sticky-Actions tables, rarely-mobile) stay as-is — already non-breaking (scroll in `overflow-x-auto`), not worth the per-table th/td regression surface for marginal admin value; ~~`admin/pricing-management` modal no `max-h`~~ ✅ mn7 (defensive `max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto`); ~~hand-rolled `min-w-[...]` cells (`admin/settings`, `CartItemCard`)~~ ✅ mn8 (`min-w-0 sm:min-w-[...]`); ~~`ChatWidget:157` uses `100vw`~~ ✅ mn9 VERIFIED SAFE (already clamped `w-[min(320px,calc(100vw-2rem))]` — no change). **Net: 7 code fixes shipped + 2 verified-already-fine + 0 deferred (mn6 later shipped surgically on the 2 main tables).**

  **Suggested remediation batches** (for when the operator greenlights fixes): **Batch A (global, highest leverage)** = C1 + M1 + M2 — a handful of lines in `globals.css`/`layout.tsx` + 2 FAB anchors, defends everywhere. **Batch B (customer-facing)** = C2 + M3 + M4 + M5 + the customer MINORs. **Batch C (admin)** = M6 + M7 + admin MINORs (desktop-first, lowest priority).

### Razorpay live-mode cutover (5-step chain, sequential — each gates the next)

- [x] **~~Razorpay live cutover — Step 1: Operator gets live API credentials + configures live webhook in Razorpay dashboard~~** — ✅ RESOLVED on 2026-06-24: dashboard prep complete. Live API credentials generated (`rzp_live_T5NRBOq7ByM414` Key Id + matching Key Secret). `https://app.anutech.in/` added to Razorpay's approved-websites list and verified ✓ (sits alongside the already-approved parent `https://anutech.in/`). Both webhook routes configured + Enabled in the live dashboard with the SAME signing secret (one webhook secret value covers both routes since the code reads from a single `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` env var): **Webhook 1** at `https://app.anutech.in/razorpay/webhook` subscribing to `payment.captured` + `refund.processed` (one-shot payment flow + refund→credit-note); **Webhook 2** at `https://app.anutech.in/api/webhooks/razorpay` subscribing to `subscription.charged` + `subscription.halted` (recurring hosting renewals + failed-renewal dunning). **Mid-step code-bug discovery + fix**: while picking dashboard event subscriptions, found the route at `app/api/webhooks/razorpay/route.ts:87` was listening for `subscription.payment_failed` — which **isn't a real Razorpay event** and has therefore been dead code since it was first written. The handler body matches `subscription.halted` semantics (expire hosting + suspend DA user + flip to manual billing on retry-exhaustion), so the fix is just renaming the event-string match. Updated across 5 files: the route's dispatch, the handler's log message, + 3 test files (unit route test, unit handler test, integration webhooks test). Tests still pin the dispatch by mocking handlers + verifying which gets called for which event-string — green after rename. **Code change is uncommitted in the working tree right now** — will ship bundled with Step 2's env-var-rotation deploy so the dashboard + code agree on the same event name from the very first live transaction. Test-mode webhook intentionally left in place for now — Step 5 takes it down once Step 4's ₹1 verification confirms live mode is healthy.

- [x] **~~Razorpay live cutover — Step 2: Rotate 4 env vars (3 in Secret Manager + 1 in `.env.local`) and redeploy~~** — ✅ RESOLVED on 2026-06-25: all 4 env vars rotated, deploy landed cleanly, every binding layer verified. **What landed**: (1) `.env.local` updated by operator with all 4 live values (public Key Id `rzp_live_T5NRBOq7ByM414` + the 3 server secrets); (4) `subscription.halted` code fix (commit `055c51a`) rode the same image so dashboard + code agree on the event name from the first live transaction. **Verification matrix** — every layer checked: Cloud Run secret bindings via `gcloud run services describe dms --format=json` show `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` / `RAZORPAY_KEY_SECRET` / `RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET` all bound to "latest" (= v2 since the new push); `NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` env shows the literal `rzp_live_T5NRBOq7ByM414`; the customer-side JS bundle at `/_next/static/chunks/app/checkout/guest/page-b53d78066180cf2e.js` contains the live key string (confirmed via curl + grep); health endpoint `https://app.anutech.in/api/health` returns `{"status":"ok"}` 200 OK; guest checkout page loads HTTP 200. **One mid-deploy fix worth noting**: gcloud was pointing at the wrong project (`boxwood-victor-453804-v4`) so the first `secrets describe` call returned `PERMISSION_DENIED`; pinned every command with explicit `--project=speedy-unison-453807-e9` instead of mutating the operator's persistent `gcloud config set project` state. **System-state caveat for the reviewer**: even though Step 2 is done, the system isn't fully live-mode-ready until Step 3 (plan regeneration) lands — see the warning paragraph in the top header. **Git audit trail**: deploy script auto-tagged + pushed `deploy-20260625-074325Z-582b907c` to origin. **Rollback path**: `dms-00205-2zv` is still the immediate fallback (RESTORE_POINTS.md Path 1).

- [x] **~~Razorpay live cutover — Step 3: Regenerate Razorpay subscription Plans for the 3 hosting tiers in live mode~~** — ✅ RESOLVED on 2026-06-25: 6 LIVE plans created, 3 HostingPlan docs rewritten, backup snapshot of pre-cutover state preserved. **What landed**: `scripts/razorpay-regenerate-plans-live.js` (new file, committed) — a one-off Node script that mirrors the price-rotation logic from `app/api/admin/hosting/packages/route.ts:296-331` (monthly amount = `renewalPrice`, yearly amount = `renewalPrice × 12`, no yearly discount applied — matches the existing PATCH-route behaviour). Script runs dry-run by default, requires explicit `--apply` for writes. Refuses to run unless `RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` starts with `rzp_live_`. Two-phase apply: all 6 Razorpay `plans.create` calls succeed in-memory FIRST, THEN the 3 `HostingPlan.save()` calls — so a Razorpay API failure mid-run leaves the DB untouched (any orphan live plans in the dashboard can be deactivated manually). Backup file refuses to overwrite an existing snapshot — guards against accidental double-runs. **Plans created in Razorpay LIVE** (timestamp 2026-06-25 ~13:39Z): **Starter** monthly=`plan_T5nEByxe0FKxP9` (₹49.99/mo) + yearly=`plan_T5nECBkYp3J9CN` (₹599.88/yr); **Standard** monthly=`plan_T5nEBcxzg8xXVz` (₹125.00/mo) + yearly=`plan_T5nEBowIdGKqIn` (₹1500.00/yr); **Plus** monthly=`plan_T5nEBAXABwtntv` (₹187.20/mo) + yearly=`plan_T5nEBQ9x8cUomF` (₹2246.40/yr). Each plan's amount is sent in paise (`Math.round(renewalPrice × 100)`) so the IEEE-754 float math doesn't corrupt the wire-level value — the cosmetic display issue in the dry-run output (`187.2 × 12 = 2246.3999…`) was fixed mid-flight via `.toFixed(2)` formatting. **Verification**: post-`--apply` re-ran the script in dry-run mode, MongoDB now shows all 3 HostingPlan docs referencing the new `plan_T5nE…` IDs (replaces the old test-mode `plan_SiQ…` / `plan_SiP…` IDs). Backup file `scripts/razorpay-plans-pre-live-backup-2026-06-25.json` (4590 bytes) preserves the 3 pre-cutover HostingPlan docs verbatim — gitignored under the new pattern `scripts/*-backup-*.json` added to `.gitignore`. **System state now fully live-mode-ready end-to-end**: both one-shot orders (domain registration, single-payment hosting) AND subscription orders (recurring hosting renewals) will work against the live Razorpay API because every layer is consistent — `NEXT_PUBLIC_RAZORPAY_KEY_ID` is live in the JS bundle, server-side `RAZORPAY_KEY_*` are live in Secret Manager v2, webhook routes are configured + Enabled for live events, `HostingPlan.razorpayPlans` references live plan IDs. The system-state warning from the Step 2 row is now resolved — removed from the top-header summary. **Step 4 (₹1 verification) can now proceed** against an end-to-end live setup.

For full revert, manually restore the 3 HostingPlan docs from `scripts/razorpay-plans-pre-live-backup-2026-06-25.json` via MongoDB shell. **Test-order recommendation**: **domain registration** (one-shot path, no subscription, no DA `valid_TLDs` dependency, no hosting provisioning to wait on — the cleanest path through the live Razorpay overlay). Pick a cheap `.com` (currently the safest TLD per the `valid_TLDs` In-Flight item — `.ai` and other newer TLDs still blocked on the new DA server). Real money: ₹1 trial isn't available for our plans, so the minimum is whatever the cheapest TLD-year combination registers at; a throwaway `.com` for ~₹800-1200/yr is the typical shape. **Verification matrix to walk through in parallel with the order placement** (5-min window — anything failing rolls back immediately): (a) order detail page in customer dashboard — payment should clear, `bookingStatus` reaches `payment_verified`, no red error banners; (b) `/admin/integration-health` → Razorpay card → stays green (signature verification passed at the webhook, no INVALID_SIGNATURE entries in SystemLog); (c) `/admin/integration-health` → Zoho Books card → stays green (invoice created via the live Zoho Books API, no `creation_failed` entries); (d) order's `zohoInvoiceId` field in MongoDB is a real Zoho ID (`70...` format), NOT `creation_failed`; (e) Zoho LIVE dashboard → Invoices → confirm the invoice exists with the right GST line item (₹X net + 18% IGST or CGST+SGST depending on the billing state); (f) Razorpay LIVE dashboard → Payments → confirm the ₹X is captured (live mode, NOT test mode); (g) Razorpay LIVE dashboard → Settlements → first transaction settles T+3 to T+5 (per Razorpay's first-live-transaction holding period; subsequent ones go to T+1/T+2). **Two judgment calls awaiting operator**: (i) **Want a streaming Integration Health monitor armed** while you place the order? Claude can `tail` the `/admin/integration-health` JSON every 5-10 seconds and emit notifications when Razorpay or Zoho card state changes — surfaces "webhook fired" / "invoice created" events in real-time, dramatically reduces "did it work?" debugging. (ii) **Test order intent** — register a real domain you'd actually want, OR use a throwaway domain you'll let expire? No code difference, just whether the ~₹800-1200 ends up being a useful expense vs a pure verification cost. **Rollback if anything fails**: traffic-shift via `RESTORE_POINTS.md` Path 1 — `gcloud run services update-traffic dms --region=europe-west1 --to-revisions=dms-00205-2zv=100 --project=speedy-unison-453807-e9` — image rolls back instantly. **Note that the Step 3 DB writes (live plan IDs in `HostingPlan.razorpayPlans`) are NOT reverted by traffic-shift alone** — for full revert run the regen script in reverse mode using `scripts/razorpay-plans-pre-live-backup-2026-06-25.json` (no automated tool for this today; manual MongoDB shell edit guided by the backup JSON if needed). **Once Step 4 passes cleanly**: Step 5 (restore-point tagging + test-webhook teardown) is the final close-out.

Test mode itself stays usable for local dev work (the test API keys still work for any dev who points their local env at them), only the webhook needs removing. **At this point the cutover is fully complete** — all real customer payments flow through live mode, Zoho invoicing in live mode, the post-cutover image is rollback-protected, and the test-mode infrastructure is cleanly torn down on the inbound side.

### Trial UX gap: Razorpay Tokens migration (design landed; implementation gated on cutover close)

Our team has been notified..." red banner. We finally surfaced the actual reason via the diagnostic chain shipped in `dms-00194-mlz` + `dms-00195-wsk`: the DirectAdmin server at `server-136-115-64-54.da.direct:2222` returned **"Cannot Execute Your Request - License is limited to 2 accounts, and you currently have 2"** on every `create-user` API call. The DA admin panel was healthy and reachable; our code was sending well-formed `create-user` requests with the correct linkedDomain (the `dms-00191-wgk` inference patch landed and works end-to-end); the IP whitelisting was intact (per the 4-layer DA whitelist auto-memory note). The blocker was purely the **DA license tier** — the original license allowed up to 2 user accounts, and there were already 2 existing accounts on the server. No code fix could have bypassed it; rather than upgrade the old server's license, the operator switched to a new DA server entirely with higher capacity. The 3 DA secrets (URL / ADMIN_USER / API_KEY) were rotated in Google Secret Manager, the service redeployed, and the next test checkout reached the new server cleanly (confirming whitelist + auth work). ## Recently Shipped — user-visible improvements

- [x] **RELIABILITY: retry sweep for failed ₹2 trial mandate refunds (money-loss gap closed)** — Diagnosed from a real test-mode trial where the dashboard showed hosting "Pending" (that was just the normal ~10-min async DA-provisioning window — it self-provisioned to active, `ftestf8abe`, no bug). The real find was the order's **`mandateRefundStatus: 'failed'`**: Razorpay showed the ₹2 payment **captured + fully refundable with 0 refunds** — so the webhook's inline refund *threw* and was never retried. Root cause: the trial webhook refunds the ₹2 mandate-validation charge **inline, once, immediately after capture**, and a just-captured recurring-auth payment can transiently reject the refund (both speeds) — with **no retry**, leaving the ₹2 permanently un-refunded (a real money-loss in **live**; in test it's fake money). **Fix (`dms-00457-rmr`):** new `retryFailedMandateRefunds()` sweep + `retry-mandate-refunds` Cloud-Scheduler worker (every 30 min) that re-attempts `mandateRefundStatus:'failed'` orders using the **real** `razorpayPaymentId` (guards against the legacy synthetic `paymentId`); **idempotent** — if Razorpay already shows a refund (a prior sweep OR a manual dashboard refund) it reconciles to `processed` instead of double-refunding. Since the payment stays refundable, a retry minutes later reliably succeeds, so transient webhook-time failures now self-heal. Job 3 added to `setup-cloud-scheduler-tokens.sh` (operator runs it to wire the schedule). 7 unit tests; tsc clean. Shipped `dms-00457-rmr` → live `dms-00457-lbk`.

- [x] **IMPROVED: "Same as WhatsApp number" is now a two-way sync (always selectable)** — Follow-up to `dms-00454` after operator feedback ("that just disabled the feature instead of fixing it"). The earlier fix *disabled* the checkbox when WhatsApp was empty to avoid wiping the phone — safe, but it dodged the point. Now the checkbox is **always clickable** and copies whichever number is already filled into the other, so the user never retypes: phone filled + WhatsApp empty → tick fills **WhatsApp** from the phone (the screenshot case); WhatsApp filled + phone empty → tick fills **Phone** from WhatsApp; both filled → WhatsApp wins (required/primary) and is copied into phone; untick clears phone so a distinct number can be entered (toggle still works). Validation on both fields recomputes from the copied value. tsc clean. Shipped `dms-00456-swa` → live `dms-00456-bk9` (supersedes the `dms-00454` stopgap).

- [x] **FIXED: social-login admins can now reset user passwords (step-up re-auth)** — Admins hit "Current password required to reset a user password" and couldn't reset anyone's password. **Root cause:** the `reset-password` route enforces step-up re-auth via `requireReAuth`, which bcrypt-compares the admin's OWN current password — but (a) the reset modal never had a field to enter it / never sent the `x-reauth-token` header, and (b) **social-login (Google) admins have no password at all**, so a password step-up is structurally impossible — they were hard-blocked from every step-up-gated action (reset-password, delete-user, admin-settings). **Operator decision (2026-08-03): session-only for passwordless admins.** Fix: `requireReAuth` now fetches the admin first and, if they have no password (social login), returns `{required:false, passed:true}` — their authenticated admin session is accepted rather than an impossible password check; password-based admins STILL must supply + match their current password. Also added the missing **"Your current password"** step-up field to the reset modal (sent as `x-reauth-token` when filled; Google admins leave it blank and are exempt). 27 admin-security + 145 requireReAuth-caller route tests pass; tsc clean. Shipped `dms-00455-rea` → live `dms-00455-928`.

- [x] **FIXED: "Same as WhatsApp number" no longer erases the phone number** — On Account Settings → Profile → Contact Numbers, ticking **"Same as WhatsApp number"** copied the WhatsApp value into Phone — but when the WhatsApp field was empty (common: WhatsApp not yet filled while a phone already is), it copied `""` and **wiped the phone the customer had already entered** (operator repro: `7778886760` → blank). The handler also only reacted to the checked=true branch, so the box could never be unticked. **Fix** (`app/dashboard/settings/page.tsx`): (1) **disable** the checkbox when there's no WhatsApp number — nothing to mirror, so it can't clobber an existing phone — with a greyed style + "Enter a WhatsApp number first" tooltip; (2) handle **both** directions — tick → phone = WhatsApp, untick → phone cleared for independent entry (the box now actually toggles); (3) clear the phone validation error on the transition. tsc clean. Shipped `dms-00454-swa` → live `dms-00454-6vc`.

- [x] **FIXED: toast notifications no longer collapse to one word per line** — Operator screenshot showed a success toast ("Starter free trial added! ₹0 today, then billed yearly after 15 days.") rendered as a ~90px-wide column, one word per line. **Root cause: a regression from the mobile-audit M1 tweak (`dms-00444`)** — it set `.toast-container { left:auto; right:80px }` at sm+ with no width; a `position:fixed` box anchored only by `right` shrinks to min-content, collapsing the container (and every toast) to the longest word, and it fought react-hot-toast's own full-width flex layout. **Fix:** stop repositioning the container — react-hot-toast owns placement via the unchanged `Toaster position="bottom-right"` + `containerStyle`, laying it out full-width so toasts size to content — and instead clamp the TOAST's own width to `min(360px, calc(100vw - 32px))`. That's the correct lever: it fixes the collapse AND the original mobile-clipping concern M1 was chasing (a toast can never exceed the viewport, so it can't be clipped off a narrow phone), while reading as one comfortable line on desktop. CSS-only. Shipped `dms-00453-tst` → live `dms-00453-fpq`.

- [x] **RESTORE-POINT-2026-08-01: clean rollback anchor before the sub-reseller feature** — Operator asked for a restore point before any sub-reseller work so everything to here can be reversed. Captured all three independent rollback paths at the current launch-ready state: **git tag** `restore-2026-08-01-pre-sub-reseller` (pushed), **Cloud Run revision** `dms-00452-z2x` (instant traffic-shift, no rebuild), and **Artifact Registry image tags** `stable-2026-08-01` + `known-good-pre-sub-reseller` on digest `sha256:042c88…be1e` (redeploy if the revision is GC'd). Recorded in `RESTORE_POINTS.md` with the exact rollback commands. This snapshot = the full backlog close-out (mobile audit + Meta Analytics + a11y gate + admin split + pending-domain tighten), Razorpay still TEST mode, no sub-reseller code.

- [x] **PENDING-DOMAIN-ID-TIGHTEN: retired a legacy loose-typed `_id` on the PendingDomain model** — Backlog tech-debt closeout. The `PendingDomain._id` was declared `Schema.Types.Mixed` ("support both ObjectId and String IDs") — a loosening that had outlived its reason. Ran the required one-time **data audit** first (read-only against prod): the collection has **0 documents** and the only persistence path auto-generates an ObjectId; the sole string ids in play are the **synthetic, order-derived rows** the admin pending-domains list builds in memory (never saved through the model). Tightened the schema back to a standard auto ObjectId `_id`, tightened the `IPendingDomain` types to match, and hardened `getPendingDomainById` to return `null` on a non-ObjectId lookup id instead of building a raw-string query (byte-for-byte the same outcome as before — those ids matched no stored doc — but without risking a Mongoose CastError on the now-strict path). No data migration was needed (empty collection). 151 pending-domains tests green (5 route suites + integration); tsc + eslint clean; the 6 unrelated failures under a "pending" name-filter are pre-existing (identical with this change stashed). Shipped `dms-00452-pid` → live `dms-00452-z2x`.

- [x] **A11Y-CI-GATE: accessibility now enforced in the build pipeline (jsx-a11y ratchet)** — Closes the "run accessibility checks automatically" backlog item. The project already had `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y` (recommended) wired up, but every rule was `warn` and CI runs `next lint --quiet`, which drops warnings — so screen-reader accessibility was configured yet silently unenforced. New `scripts/check-a11y.mjs` (`npm run lint:a11y`) runs those same rules, prints every violation grouped by rule, and fails CI only when the total exceeds a frozen baseline. That makes it a **ratchet**: any newly-introduced a11y violation turns the build red, while the pre-existing debt stays visible and can only trend down (lower `A11Y_BASELINE` after each cleanup to lock the gain in). Wired as its own CI step after lint. Baseline captured at 141 and immediately dropped to 139 by fixing two real issues — the `<html>` on the global error page now carries `lang="en"`, and the reusable `CardTitle` heading got a justified scoped-disable (its content always comes from consumer `{...props}`, a static-analysis false positive). Failure + pass paths both verified. The bulk of the remaining 139 is `label-has-associated-control` (form inputs needing associated labels) — now capped and trackable. Shipped `dms-00451-a11` → live `dms-00451-qsj`.

- [x] **ADMIN-PAGE-SPLIT (safe pass): extracted types + primitives out of the largest admin pages** — Maintainability refactor of the 5 biggest admin `page.tsx` files (1k–2k lines each). Operator picked the **safe-mechanical-only** scope because these are operator-critical pages that can't be exercised in this environment (need auth + live data) — so the split is limited to relocations that *cannot* change behaviour and are fully caught by the type-checker: co-located TypeScript interfaces → `./types.ts`, and (for settings) the already-module-scope pure presentational primitives (`SCard`, `SCardHead`, `Toggle`, `SaveBtn`, `StatusBanner`, `SFooter`, `SettingsContentSkeleton`) + the `previewTrackingId` helper → `./primitives.tsx`. Net: hosting −43, user-management −11, dns-management −37 (incl. removing a dead `NameserverInfo` type), settings −124 lines; system-settings had nothing top-level to pull out. tsc clean; eslint clean; no logic touched, no runtime change. The deeper (and genuinely valuable) stateful-component extraction — pulling the big tables + detail/create modals into their own components — was **not** attempted here: it carries real regression risk and must be verified by loading each admin page, which needs an app run this environment can't do. Shipped `dms-00450-sp1`+`sp2` → live `dms-00450-vh7`.

- [x] **MOBILE-MINOR-POLISH: cleared the mobile audit's 10 MINOR polish items (one commit each)** — Worked the deferred MINOR tier one at a time. **6 shipped as code** (`dms-00448-mn1..mn8`): mn1 register First/Last name fields now stack on phones (`grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2`); mn2 invoice download/view icon buttons meet the 44px tap target (`p-1.5`→`p-2.5`); mn3 Order History header stacks (`flex-col` → `sm:flex-row`); mn5 admin DNS sidebar's `h-[calc(100vh-14rem)] sticky` gated to `xl:` so it's natural-height when stacked; mn7 pricing-management cache-purge overlay got a defensive `max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto`; mn8 relaxed hand-rolled `min-w-[…]` cells (CartItemCard price/period chips + admin WhatsApp-test input) to `min-w-0 sm:min-w-[…]`. **2 verified already-fine (no change, honest audit):** mn4 support stat tiles (3 compact count-chips read correctly at 3-across — a 2+1 split would look worse) and mn9 ChatWidget width (already clamped `w-[min(320px,calc(100vw-2rem))]`). **1 deferred:** mn6 the `hidden sm:table-cell` column-drop on a few raw admin tables — a multi-table refactor needing per-table column-priority calls; admin-only and non-breaking today (tables already scroll in `overflow-x-auto`), so not worth the regression risk in a polish pass. All localized CSS/className changes; tsc clean; no logic touched. Live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00448-rlg` (100% traffic, /api/health 200 OK). **Follow-up `dms-00449-j5l`:** mn6 (the deferred admin table column-drop) then shipped surgically too — admin domains + hosting main list tables now hide their most-secondary column below sm (`hidden sm:table-cell` on th + matching td, verified balanced), leaving the in-modal / sticky-Actions / rarely-mobile tables as-is (already non-breaking). **This closes the entire 2026-07-17 mobile audit — CRITICAL + MAJOR + all 10 MINOR — with nothing outstanding.**

- [x] **SYNC-PATH-CONVERSION: hosting conversions now fire on every provisioning path (closes all Meta Analytics follow-ups)** — The synchronous (non-tokens) provisioner walks a paid order's cart and provisions hosting via `provisionHostingItem`, but — unlike its domain sibling (`recordDomainProvisioned`) and the tokens-flow cron (`recordHostingProvisioned`) — it fired **no** conversion event. So if `HOSTING_MANDATE_FLOW` ever moved off `tokens`, synchronously-provisioned hosting would silently stop reporting StartTrial/Purchase to Meta + the internal funnel — a latent analytics blind spot. Fix: capture the created Hosting doc and fire `recordHostingProvisioned(_id)` fire-and-forget on success, reusing the same helper the tokens path uses. That helper carries an **atomic `conversionEventSent` claim** + a deterministic `event_id`, so this can never double-count even if two paths ever touched one hosting, and Meta dedups the browser/server twin. Best-effort — a tracking failure never affects provisioning. 3 new tests; full payment suite 262/262. Shipped `dms-00447-shp` → **live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00447-rf4`** (100% traffic, `/api/health` 200 OK).

- [x] **BEACON-RATE-LIMIT: hardened the public analytics beacon (Meta Phase-4 follow-up)** — With the CAPI-dedup work (`dms-00443`), a `view_content` / `checkout_started` hit on the unauthenticated `/api/analytics/track` beacon now also triggers a Meta Conversions API send + a `CustomerActivity` write — meaning an unbounded public endpoint could be scripted to flood Mongo + Meta. Added an `analyticsBeacon` rate limiter (reusing the shared `lib/rate-limit` infra) checked as the **first, cheapest gate** before any JWT / DB / CAPI work. Deliberately generous at **120/min/IP** so real browsing — including many users behind one CGNAT / office IP — is never affected (a dropped beacon is a missed event, never broken UX; the send is fire-and-forget), and it fails open on a Redis outage like every other limiter. This closes the last genuine engineering item under Meta Analytics Phase 4 — reporting is now both resilient (server-CAPI dedup) AND abuse-bounded. 1 new test (429 + no DB write + no CAPI on throttle); tsc clean. Shipped `dms-00446-arl` → **live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00446-qb5`** (100% traffic, `/api/health` 200 OK).

- [x] **MOBILE-AUDIT-REMEDIATION: phones no longer horizontal-scroll; customer + admin responsive fixes** — Applied the 2026-07-17 mobile-compatibility audit's every CRITICAL + MAJOR finding across two focused batches (360–414px phones). **Batch A+B (`dms-00444-z5s`, customer-facing):** (C1) a one-line global `html,body{overflow-x:hidden;max-width:100%}` safety net so no stray over-wide element can make a whole page scroll sideways — highest-leverage fix, defends every page; (C2) the bulk-domain-search result rows packed ~419px of fixed-width children and forced horizontal scroll on a 360px screen — now `flex-wrap` + `min-w-0` domain + responsive gutters; (M1) toast notifications were anchored `right:80px` and clipped off-screen on phones — now mobile-first full-width (16px gutters, lifted above the chat FAB), restoring the corner anchor at sm+; (M2) the scroll-to-top FAB now hides until `md` so it never collides with the bottom-left chat widget; (M3) the hosting-upgrade modal could clip its Pay/Confirm button off a short phone — now `max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto`; (M4) the domain-detail nameserver option cards now stack (`flex-col sm:flex-row`) instead of crushing to ~150px; (M5) a long domain name in the domain-detail header now truncates (`min-w-0`) instead of shoving the status pill. **Batch C (commit `dms-00444-cba` → live `dms-00445-fb2`, admin, lowest priority):** (M6) the DNS-records Save/Cancel/Edit/Delete icon buttons went p-1.5→p-2.5 for 44px tap targets; (M7) the DNS "Value" column dropped its forced `min-w-[200px]` on mobile so the contained table stops forcing operators to side-scroll (desktop min-width restored at sm+). No systemic breakage existed (the app was already mobile-first — drawer sidebars, overflow-x tables, proper viewport meta) so all fixes were localized CSS/className changes; tsc clean, no new test failures. The 10 MINOR polish items are deferred (cosmetic, non-breaking).

- [x] **FIXED: Meta Pixel events resilient to ad-blockers (server-CAPI dedup twin)** — Resolves the long-open "browser Pixel events don't appear in Events Manager" report. The Pixel was correctly installed all along (CSP, snippet, dataset id, token all verified) — the miss is that browser `/tr` beacons are blocked for 20–40% of visitors (ad-blockers, iOS/Safari ITP), which is a client-side reality no install fix can change. **The fix stops depending on the browser beacon:** the two mid-funnel events (`ViewContent` on /hosting, `InitiateCheckout` on /checkout) now ALSO fire server-side through the Meta Conversions API, sharing a single `event_id` with the browser event so Meta deduplicates them — ad-blocked users still get counted (server leg), non-blocked users collapse to one event. Real `_fbp`/`_fbc` cookies are forwarded from the browser for match quality; client IP / user-agent / event-source-URL come from the request. A strict activity→event map means a client can't fire an arbitrary Meta event (e.g. Purchase) from the public beacon; `start_trial` stays server-provisioning-only (never counts an unfulfilled click) and `PageView` stays browser-only. `StartTrial`/`Purchase`/`CompleteRegistration` already had server CAPI, so this closes the remaining gap — every conversion-relevant event now has a server path. Files: `lib/journey.ts` (shared event_id + cookie forwarding), `app/api/analytics/track/route.ts` (dedup twin), `lib/meta-capi.ts` (new event names + real fbp/fbc). 6 new tests; no new failures vs baseline. Also corrected a stale backlog note: of the "5 unwired journey events", 3 were already wired and 2 (`wordpress_installed`/`website_uploaded`) have no in-app flow to hook (they happen inside the DA panel). Shipped `dms-00443-mca` → **deployed live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00443-29x`** (100% traffic, `/api/health` 200 OK, deploy tag `deploy-20260730-052619Z-f943abac`). Note: gcloud auth expired mid-push on the first attempt; operator re-ran `gcloud auth login` and the pre-built image pushed + promoted cleanly on retry.

- [x] **HARDENED: domain guard is now airtight (shared hostname validator)** — Follow-up to the fix below after an "is this really airtight?" review. The first cut used `startsWith("hosting-") || !includes(".")`, which had two holes: it (a) **false-rejected** real registrable domains that start with `hosting-` (e.g. `hosting-guru.com`, `hosting-24.net`), and (b) **false-accepted** malformed hostnames (`.com`, `a.`, `x. y`, `localhost`, `http://example.com`, `example..com`) that DA would still reject — re-opening the same charge-then-fail hole. Replaced both create-order routes' inline check with a shared, unit-tested validator `lib/validation/hosting-domain.ts` using a proper IDN/punycode-aware hostname regex. Verified the **whole payment gate** is covered: (1) `/api/v1/*` rewrites to `/api/*` (next.config), so both guarded route files ARE the endpoints the logged-in + guest checkouts actually call — no un-guarded `v1` twin; (2) the guard runs **after** single-domain inference and **before** every trial / subscription / tokens / manual branch, so **no** hosting order (paid or trial) can be created without a provisionable domain. 9 new helper tests (incl. the `hosting-guru.com`-accepted and placeholder-rejected cases) + existing route tests green; no new failures vs baseline. Shipped `dms-00442-k3p` → **deployed live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00442-rbk`** (100% traffic, `/api/health` 200 OK, deploy tag `deploy-20260729-065012Z-ec8e1670`).

- [x] **FIXED: paid hosting requires a real domain — no more charge-then-fail provisioning** — Chose **Option A** for the In-Flight "paid hosting without a linked domain fails to provision" issue: a customer can no longer buy hosting without connecting a domain. Both `create-order` and `guest/create-order` now (1) infer the linked domain when the cart holds exactly one domain item, then (2) reject the whole order up front with a clear 400 `HOSTING_DOMAIN_REQUIRED` — *"Please connect a domain to your hosting plan before checking out. Link a domain you already own, or register a new one — hosting can't be set up without a domain."* — if any hosting item still has no real domain (empty / `hosting-` placeholder / no dot). Full coverage: the logged-in cart already blocks unlinked hosting client-side, and the guest checkout surfaces the backend 400 as a toast + resets state (no stuck payment screen). Trials fall under the same guard (a trial without a domain would fail DA provisioning identically). This closes the case where a guest paid ₹2246.40 + got INV-000030 but no hosting. No new test failures introduced (verified against the pre-existing baseline); test fixtures updated to link a real domain. Shipped `dms-00441-d7x` → **deployed live as Cloud Run revision `dms-00441-w8x`** (100% traffic, smoke test `/api/health` 200 OK, deploy tag `deploy-20260729-063008Z-63b34c75`).

- [x] **FIXED: guest checkout now always sends the set-password email** — A guest who paid got an account but **no password-setup email**, so they could never activate/sign in. Cause: the setup-email block sat AFTER the order-claim + provisioning, but guest-verify returns early ("provisioning in progress") when the `/razorpay/webhook` wins the provisioning race — skipping the email entirely (confirmed on `careerlamps2023`: `isGuest:true` but `resetToken` never set, no email). Moved the send to **right after `createUser`, before the claim**, so it fires for every new guest regardless of the verify-vs-webhook race AND regardless of whether hosting provisioning succeeds (they paid → they must always be able to set a password). Fire-and-forget; never fails verification. 42 guest-verify tests pass. Shipped `dms-00440-9xk`.

- [x] **OPS: test reset + Razorpay token cleanup for same-email re-testing; guest-checkout behavior documented (no deploy, 2026-07-28)** — Support work for the operator's repeated test cycles. (1) Reset `careerlamps2023@gmail.com` again via the standard scoped delete (DA account + hosting + order + TrialClaim + 13 CustomerActivity + user; verified 0 remaining, 0 trialclaims anywhere so neither the account nor device throttle trips). (2) Cleared **3 stale recurring card tokens** (all `status:failed`) on the reused Razorpay customer `cust_TIRfyMvwxnO5AK` (`DELETE /customers/{id}/tokens/{id}` → 0 remaining) — they were causing "Invalid Token" on checkout after the trial-cancel token-revoke. (3) **Documented two guest-checkout facts for the reviewer:** (a) guest checkout **intentionally blocks trials** (`checkout/guest/page.tsx` guard: "Free trials require an account" — the one-trial-per-account limit needs an account anchor; recommended keeping this, or building inline account-creation-at-trial-checkout if signup friction is the concern — NOT anonymous trials); (b) the guest paid checkout's "Invalid Token" was a Razorpay-side test-mode artifact (real UPI QR can't pay a test order; and a contact-level saved card `DCB •1007` backed by a revoked token) — resolved by using a fresh test card. No code change; the trial + paid-guest flows themselves are working.

- [x] **CANCEL-TRIAL: sends a cancellation confirmation email** — Cancelling a free trial did the full teardown (terminate hosting + suspend DA + revoke mandate token) but sent NO email, so the customer got no confirmation of the account state change (verified live: the cancel worked, backend correct — hosting `terminated`, `autoRenew:false`, token CLEARED — but no mail). Added `sendHostingTrialCancelledEmail` (service/transactional — sends regardless of marketing opt-out) confirming the trial is cancelled, hosting terminated, **no charge taken**, and the mandate cancelled, plus a "changed your mind? contact support" line. Wired into `cancel-trial` best-effort (a mail failure never fails the cancellation). +1 test (18 cancel-trial tests pass); verified by sending the live template to a test inbox. Shipped `dms-00439-s48`.

- [x] **CANCEL-TRIAL: now revokes the Razorpay mandate token (tokens-flow teardown)** — Cancelling a Tokens-flow trial terminated the hosting + suspended DA + set `autoRenew=false`, but left the stored mandate **token orphaned** — `cancel-trial` only cancelled a Razorpay *subscription*, which tokens trials don't have (they have a `razorpayTokenId`). Added `RazorpayService.revokeToken(customerId, tokenId)` (best-effort `customers.deleteToken`; a failure never blocks cancellation) and wired it into `cancel-trial`: revoke when `razorpayTokenId` + `razorpayCustomerId` are set, then clear `razorpayTokenId` so we never point at a dead token. Cancellation is now a complete teardown — nothing left dangling in Razorpay. (Not a correctness bug before — the terminated + `autoRenew=false` hosting already stopped the recurring cron — just cleaner.) Also fixed a pre-existing `refundPayment` test that mocked one rejection though the dms-00418 optimum→normal fallback calls refund twice. +2 revoke tests; 17 cancel-trial + 15 razorpay-tokens tests pass. Shipped `dms-00438-j26`.

- [x] **HOSTING: removed the auto-renewal toggle from the regular-user dashboard (always-on for mandate plans)** — Operator: regular customers shouldn't be able to disable auto-renewal. For mandate-billed (subscription/tokens) plans it's intrinsically always-on — the saved Razorpay mandate renews the plan automatically, so a per-row "off" toggle was misleading (it wouldn't stop the mandate). First locked the toggle to a non-clickable "on" (`dms-00436-gws`), then per follow-up **removed the Auto-Renewal control entirely** from the dashboard hosting row (`dms-00437-mjk`) — that line now shows only "DA User". Backend enforcement stays: the user auto-renew API rejects `autoRenew=false` for `billingType='subscription'` (defence-in-depth vs a direct API call), and tokens trials already default `autoRenew=true`. To stop future billing the customer cancels the plan (a separate flow), matching the mandate model. +2 auto-renew route tests (17 pass). Shipped `dms-00437-mjk`.

- [x] **FIXED: CSP no longer blocks the Meta Pixel's form-POST fallback** — Console showed `Sending form data to https://www.facebook.com/tr/ violates … form-action 'self' …`. The Meta Pixel (`fbevents.js`) falls back to submitting a hidden `<form>` to `facebook.com/tr` when its img/fetch beacon can't be used, but the CSP `form-action` directive listed only `'self'` + Razorpay + Google — not Facebook — so the browser blocked it and that Pixel event was lost (a few conversions could go unrecorded in Meta). Added `https://www.facebook.com` to `form-action` in `lib/security/headers.ts` — consistent with `connect-src`/`img-src`/`script-src`, which already allow Facebook for the Pixel. Fires on any page with the Pixel (unrelated to the trial flow). Security-headers tests 15/15. Shipped `dms-00435-52t`.

- [x] **FIXED (round 2): tokens-trial /verify no longer 400s — short-circuit moved ahead of ALL verification** — A 2nd trial retest (testingwithme.com) again showed `POST /verify 400` after a successful mandate. The dms-00423 fix ran the tokens-trial branch AFTER strict validation + signature/amount checks, so a different gate could still reject first (the required `cartItems`, or the mandate's missing/oddly-signed signature) — and prod `serverLogger` is silenced so the exact gate wasn't visible. Rather than chase a silenced error, restructured /verify: look the order up ONCE up front, ownership-check it, and short-circuit `hosting_trial` + `tokens` orders to success BEFORE `verifyRazorpayPayment` — the `/razorpay/webhook payment.captured` handler (RAZORPAY_WEBHOOK_SECRET-authenticated) is the authoritative, idempotent verifier and already completed the order. Also dropped the non-empty `cartItems` requirement (pending orders derive items from the DB; the tokens trial is finalised by the webhook), so an omitted/empty cart no longer 400s. Covers BOTH the validation-gate and signature-gate failure paths. Backend chain verified working again independently (order completed, ₹2 refunded, hosting active as `testi4c7f4`) — this only fixes the customer-facing confirmation. 42 verify tests pass (3 updated for new behavior). Shipped `dms-00434-cw2`.

- [x] **FIXED: Account Settings → Billing tab "Save Changes" buttons now act per-section too** — Follow-up to the Profile fix: the Billing & Address tab had the same shared-state issue — "Business Details" (company/GST) and "Address" each had a Save button on one `isDirty`/`isSaving`, so editing either lit up + spun both. Added `businessDirty` + `addressDirty` flags and extended `savingSection` to `'business'`/`'address'`, so each card's button only activates + spins for its own edits. Now **all four** account-settings cards (identity, contact, business, address) save independently; still one persist call. Typecheck clean. Shipped `dms-00433-47r`.

- [x] **FIXED: Account Settings → Profile "Save Changes" buttons now act per-section** — Operator: clicking one "Save Changes" made "both save changes happen." Cause: the Personal Information and Contact Numbers cards each had a Save button, but both shared ONE `isDirty`/`isSaving` state + ONE handler — so editing any field lit up BOTH buttons and clicking either showed both spinning. Added per-section dirty flags (identity = first/last name; contact = whatsapp/phone/whatsapp-optout/email-optout) computed against the last-saved snapshot, plus a `savingSection` indicator so only the clicked card's button activates + spins. The whole user record is still persisted in one call (the server requires the WhatsApp number, so an identity-only payload isn't possible) — this fixes the confusing UX without changing what's saved. Billing tab intentionally left unchanged (same shared pattern, but not reported; no regression). Typecheck clean. Shipped `dms-00432-fdn`.

(2) Removed the "GSTIN: 07ABDCA0298H1ZP" line from **every** email footer — 4 occurrences across `auth.ts` (welcome + 2 footers) + `domain.ts`.
